# Firefox Companion feature parity

## How to use this document

Firefox Companion is a working name (decision D9) for an original Firefox sidebar with Claude plus an MCP bridge, for stable Firefox on Windows 11. Claude works only with tabs the user grants, in the user's existing signed-in profile. Claude in Chrome (source 24) is the workflow benchmark. The 23 other sources show what has already been done on Firefox.

Prompt 2 built the Windows browser bridge slice (2026-11-24). Rows with the status `implemented` or `partial` name what was shown. Everything else is not built. The document says what to build, what each source showed, and how to check the result. [project-state.md](project-state.md) is the handoff of the code.

Read it in this order:

1. "Permission boundaries" and "Platform prerequisites" hold for every row. If a row seems to disagree with them, they win.
2. "Decisions" lists the choices the user approved on 2026-11-24, the scope of each run, and the acceptance criteria of Prompt 2. Rows follow them.
3. "Capability matrix" is the work list. One row is one observable capability.
4. Open a source only for a specific implementation question, and only at the pinned commit.

How to read a matrix row:

- ID: `FC-AREA-NN`. Areas are TAB, READ, ACT, SHOT, DBG, DATA, GRANT, CONN, UI, AGT and INST.
- Observable behavior: what to build, with limits and error replies. Where a source got something wrong, the row names the finding so that the mistake is not repeated.
- Sources: finding IDs, strongest reference first. [10-F05](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/host/src/tools.js#L21-L24) is feature row 5 of source 10. Each ID links to its first piece of evidence at the pinned commit. The full finding is in `docs/audit/NN-*.md`.
- Target: components from "Architecture and component map", and the MCP tool name in backticks. Tool names are proposals.
- Acceptance test: one check that can fail. "Fixture page" means a local test page under `test/`.
- Status: `implemented` (the acceptance test of the row was shown), `partial` (what was shown, then what is missing or open), `planned`, `planned-adapted`, `deferred`, `excluded` or `platform-blocked`, with a reason for all but `planned`. Evidence letters: U unit test with a mocked browser, I integration test on real Windows pipes with Firefox played by the test, F real Firefox 160.0.1 with a disposable profile and the temporary add-on.

Totals, computed from the tables of this document and of `docs/audit/`:

- Findings: 913 in 24 sources (770 verified, 134 partial, 9 claimed-only). 877 are in the Sources cells of the matrix, and the other 36 are in "Findings not carried as capabilities".
- FC rows: 263. Implemented 6, partial 47, planned 132, planned-adapted 40, deferred 19, excluded 17, platform-blocked 2.
- MCP tool names in implemented, partial, planned and planned-adapted rows: 71.
- Benchmark findings of source 24: 48. Coverage full 15, partial 19, different 12, none 2.

## Pinned sources and licenses

Audit date: 2026-11-23. Nothing from a source may be copied without meeting its license. The plan is original code (decision D8), so these obligations apply only if a later run decides to copy something.

- MIT: keep the copyright notice and the license text with every copied part.
- Apache-2.0: keep the license text and the NOTICE text, and mark changed files.
- MPL-2.0 (03 and 11): copyleft per file. A file that contains copied or modified MPL code stays under MPL-2.0 and its source must be available.
- GPL-3.0: the Bibata cursor shapes in 11 must not be reused.
- Source 24 is proprietary. It is a behavior reference only. No code, text or artwork may be copied.

## Platform prerequisites

Target: Firefox 160.0.1 x64, release channel, on Windows 11 build 26300. All facts were retrieved on 2026-11-23 from these pins:

- BCD: [mdn/browser-compat-data at 2f696a25](https://github.com/mdn/browser-compat-data/tree/2f696a2565efa5303ffd9e6a13365a0ef1a4f114/webextensions)
- MDN: [mdn/content at e4ffa5e4](https://github.com/mdn/content/tree/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions)
- EW (Extension Workshop): [mozilla/extension-workshop at c5927241](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/tree/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation)

### Behavior and policy facts

| ID | Fact | Pinned source | What it means here |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | Extensions must be signed by Mozilla through AMO to install in release and beta Firefox, also when self-distributed. Unsigned installs need Developer Edition, Nightly or ESR with a preference. | [EW signing L51](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/signing-and-distribution-overview.md#L51) [L70](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/signing-and-distribution-overview.md#L70) | Every release build goes through AMO signing. |
| P2 | Add-on policies apply to listed and self-distributed add-ons. Add-ons must be self-contained and must not load remote code for execution. | [EW policies L156](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies.md#L156) | No agent-supplied page JavaScript in the signed build (D5). |
| P3 | Add-ons must not relax page security headers such as CSP. | [EW policies L158](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies.md#L158) | Page hooks use the content-script bridge, never a CSP change. |
| P4 | The userScripts API is allowed for user script managers only. | [EW policies L177](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies.md#L177) | userScripts is not a way around P2. |
| P5 | Data sent to a native application counts as data transmission and needs consent. Transmission of browsing activity is allowed only for the primary function. | [EW policies L198](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies.md#L198) [preview L212](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies-preview-2026-12.md#L212) | Page data goes to the host only for tabs the user granted. |
| P6 | New extensions must declare data collection with the built-in consent system (data_collection_permissions). | [EW data consent L29](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/develop/firefox-builtin-data-consent.md#L29) | The manifest declares each data type. |
| P7 | If an add-on name uses "Firefox", the expected form is `<Add-on name> for Firefox`. The name "Firefox Companion" does not follow it. | [EW policies L89](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies.md#L89) | Decision D9. |
| P8 | A policy revision takes effect on 2026-12-01 and keeps P2, P3, P4, P5 and P7. | [preview L31](https://github.com/mozilla/extension-workshop/blob/c5927241c03d376b87e53613821b3e73d3181e31/src/content/documentation/publish/add-on-policies-preview-2026-12.md#L31) | Check the revision again before the first submission. |
| P9 | Manifest V3 backgrounds are event pages. Manifest V2 may be persistent. Message ports cannot keep an event page alive. | [MDN background L24](https://github.com/mdn/content/blob/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions/background_scripts/index.md#L24) [L227](https://github.com/mdn/content/blob/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions/background_scripts/index.md#L227) | Decision D1. |
| P10 | Each browser window has its own sidebar document, and a panel can be set per tab. | [MDN sidebars L18](https://github.com/mdn/content/blob/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions/user_interface/sidebars/index.md#L18) | Sidebar state lives in the background page, not in the sidebar document. |
| P11 | Per-user native host registration on Windows is the key HKEY_CURRENT_USER\SOFTWARE\Mozilla\NativeMessagingHosts\name, whose value is the manifest path. allowed_extensions lists add-on IDs. | [MDN native manifests L380](https://github.com/mdn/content/blob/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions/native_manifests/index.md#L380) | The installer needs no administrator rights. |
| P12 | A native message to the extension is at most 1 MB and a message to the host at most 4 GB. | [MDN native messaging L240](https://github.com/mdn/content/blob/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions/native_messaging/index.md#L240) | Uploads go to the extension in chunks. Screenshots to the host need no chunking. |
| P13 | On Windows Firefox puts the native host in a Job object and kills it with the job, so children that must outlive it need to break away. | [MDN native messaging L387](https://github.com/mdn/content/blob/e4ffa5e4befde66732cad271e39154a80477a33c/files/en-us/mozilla/add-ons/webextensions/native_messaging/index.md#L387) | A chat run started by the host ends with Firefox unless it breaks away. |

Not verified in the audit: behavior on a running Firefox 160 (Prompt 2 checked the points below), how AMO reviewers judge agent-supplied JavaScript or an AI agent that uses native messaging, and whether a claude.ai subscription may back a third-party sidebar.

Two consequences that many rows depend on:

- Capture needs more than a per-origin host permission. tabs.captureTab needs `<all_urls>`, and tabs.captureVisibleTab needs `<all_urls>` or activeTab. Without `<all_urls>`, a screenshot works only for the visible tab on which the user has just used the toolbar button or the context menu. Confirmed on Firefox 160.0.1 for the toolbar button, until the tab navigates.
- Input events made by a content script carry `isTrusted: false`. Pages that check this can ignore them. Keys dispatched this way do not trigger browser default actions, and hover does not set the CSS `:hover` state. Confirmed on Firefox 160.0.1 for clicks (`isTrusted` is false). Such a click on a link with target=_blank does open a tab, in the foreground.
- Host permissions carry no port: the prompt for http://localhost:3000 asks for "sites in localhost domains". Confirmed on Firefox 160.0.1. Grants therefore compare the exact origin as well.

## Permission boundaries

### What the sources do

| # | Grant model | Authentication and bind | Sensitive-action gating | Main gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 01 | MCP calls: none, any tab ID or the active tab. Sidebar chats: an exact-hostname allow list or a global "acts without asking" mode | Per-user Unix socket with mode 0600, no token. Native messaging limited to the add-on ID. No TCP | A card in the sidebar with Allow once, Always and Deny, for chat actions only | The MCP path skips the card and lists every tab. The card takes focus when no text field is focused, so one key press can approve ([01-F17](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/extension/chat.js#L494-L536)) |
| 02 | Per-origin optional host permission, checked in most handlers. Navigate, select, close, list and resize skip it | WebSocket on 127.0.0.1 and ::1 (all interfaces under CONTAINERIZED). Signed control frames, or admission by Origin | Automation Mode for 33 commands, per-tool switches, deny list, audit log of 100 entries | Peers admitted by Origin exchange unsigned frames and receive typed text and upload bytes. Cookies without a URL skip the site check |
| 03 | None. Any tab in any connected profile; the focused tab is the default | HTTP on the host from config.json (default 127.0.0.1, not checked), bearer token, every Origin refused, Host checked | None enforced. Password inputs are redacted in two read tools | Bind host unchecked, response bodies unredacted, any readable file can be uploaded, an unknown port 9222 is trusted |
| 04 | Ownership by agent ID for pool tabs. Any user tab can be attached with no prompt | Unix socket or Windows named pipe with a per-start token file (0600, icacls). The pipe itself gets no ACL | Script execution off by default, owner check on close | Attached tabs are open to every agent. Consent buttons are clicked without asking. Typing echoes password values ([04-F05](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L743-L771)) |
| 05 | None per tab or site. Category switches, pause, blocked sites | WebSocket on localhost limited to moz-extension origins, HMAC pairing. The HTTP MCP port has no authentication in the repository | Page scripts off by default, clipboard rule, blocked sites | Blocked sites do not cover tab lists, moves, groups and monitors. `test.*` actions bypass the settings |
| 06 | Session lease per tab, taken without a user prompt. Blacklist popup, empty by default | Named pipe or Unix socket, host token in the first frame, plain comparison, no ACL on Windows. A dialog hook on 127.0.0.1 HTTP with a token the page can read | Blacklist popup only | A default install gates no site. Tab lists and history are unfiltered. A confirmation answer is accepted from any extension sender that has a pending ID |
| 07 | No lasting grant. Each acting call needs approval bound to tool, site and argument hash | Loopback daemon with HMAC, nonce and timestamp or an API key, and a mutual challenge on the WebSocket. Native messaging | Rate limit, scheme and URL lists, read-only mode, approval, masked fields | `/health` is open, no Origin check on HTTP, approval is per hostname, and a process with the API token can answer its own request |
| 08 | Host allow list and block list. An empty allow list means every tab | HTTP long poll on 127.0.0.1 with Host, Origin and constant-time token checks | Pause, read-only mode, script switch, password refusal in typing | `press_key` types into password fields ([08-F12](https://github.com/jhueby/claude-browser-bridge/blob/58749d978a26d7b675225d15d6b17591034ab936/server/bridge.py#L331-L332)). Any moz-extension origin is accepted |
| 09 | None. One on and off switch | WebSocket on 127.0.0.1, moz-extension Origin, mutual HMAC login. A plaintext legacy login is still on ([09-F22](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/plugin/server/bridge.js#L403)) | Secret fields refused in typing, keys and form input. Link scheme check | Navigate, select and close act on browser pages ([09-F05](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/plugin/server/bridge.js#L733-L737), [09-F06](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/plugin/server/bridge.js#L739-L743), [09-F07](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/plugin/server/bridge.js#L745-L749)). The log keeps key arguments |
| 10 | Session owns its window or group. No user grant per tab or site | Unix socket with directory and file modes, or a Windows named pipe where the host proves a key. The host does not authenticate clients. No TCP | None. Secret fields masked, fixed dialog answers, host guard on actions | The pipe key file has no explicit ACL. Any readable file can be uploaded. Hover skips the guard ([10-F08](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/extension/background/tools/computer.js#L98-L129)) |
| 11 | None. Any tab by ID or the active tab | HTTP on a configurable address (default 127.0.0.1) with a bearer token, Host and Origin checks | `confirm: true` on four destructive tools, pause, dialogs cancelled by default | A wildcard bind leaves the token as the only control. Storage and cookie reads are unfiltered. URL scheme not limited |
| 12 | None. Any tab by ID or the active tab | HTTP and WebSocket, default 127.0.0.1 but not enforced, API key compared with `==`, no Origin or Host check | Rate limit, scheme and block lists, confirmation on protected sites through a banner in the page | Reads return password values ([12-F06](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/content.js#L1466-L1481), [12-F11](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/content.js#L895-L961), [12-F12](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/content.js#L964-L976)). The approval banner is in page DOM. Observation tools skip the check |

| 14 | Optional script consent per tab and origin, off by default. Nothing else | WebSocket limited to moz-extension origins, loopback peer and Host. No shared secret. The MCP HTTP port has no authentication | Script consent, predefined scripts limited to one directory | Any local process can call every tool. `/proxy` forwards test actions that delete history |
| 15 | Session acts only on tabs it opened or grouped. No user grant | None. The WebSocket listens on all interfaces with no Origin check | A user hand-off that blocks AI actions until Done | Unauthenticated port on every interface. The DOM read returns password values ([15-F06](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/bridge-server/mcp-server.js#L277-L286)) |
| 16 | The agent claims tabs by itself. A fixed list of restricted hosts | Daemon on 127.0.0.1, token in the query for clients, a signed challenge and six-digit pairing for the extension | Pairing, Stop, credential-named data hidden in transcripts | `tab list` shows every web tab and `tab select` claims any of them. `get value` returns passwords ([16-F17](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/commands.ts#L506-L549)). Status route needs no token |

Domain approval in the default mode | WebSocket on localhost with HMAC-signed messages both ways and a moz-extension Origin check | Pause, per-tool switches, deny list, file approval, card-number refusal | The documented per-tab screenshot consent is not enforced. Pause does not stop a running call. Refused calls are not logged |
| 18 | Per-origin optional host permission for text and find. Toolbar click per tab for screenshots | HMAC-signed messages, no Origin check, 127.0.0.1 and ::1 (all interfaces under CONTAINERIZED) | Per-tool switches, deny list, log of allowed calls | A web page can open the socket and become the command target. Listing, closing and moving need no grant |
| 19 | None | None. WebSocket and HTTP on 127.0.0.1:8787 with no token or Origin check | None | Unauthenticated command endpoint. Page reads return cookies and storage ([19-F06](https://github.com/sinamohsenifar/foxbridge-mcp/blob/b46603599a437f4918eae0256fecb78ab2576141/src/index.ts#L169-L185)) |
| 20 | None | None. 127.0.0.1 by default; `--host` accepts any address | None | Any client replaces the extension. Page script can forge console entries |

| 22 | None inside its own separate profile | Bearer token on 127.0.0.1, every Origin refused | Upload size and count limits | It is not the user's profile. Script execution is unrestricted |

The console is hooked in every tab before any grant ([23-F13](https://github.com/nullthrix/firefox-mcp-server/blob/f678ce9c11f2d9eb02b2c4f56a86c948558b127f/extension/manifest.json#L46-L56)). Kills whatever process holds the port |
| 24 | The user approves a site; the tools work only in the session's tab group | claude.ai login; the rest is not stated | Asks before purchases and irreversible actions (not listed), plan-mode prompts, upload path rules | Auto mode can skip the per-site check unless a rule denies the site |

### Firefox Companion permission model

Rows refer to these rules as PM1 to PM10.

- PM1 Grant. A grant is made only by a user gesture in the sidebar, the tab context menu or the toolbar. It names a tab, an origin and a level (read or act). It lasts for the tab session, that is until the tab closes or the session ends (FC-GRANT-06), unless the user shortens it. References: [23-F01](https://github.com/nullthrix/firefox-mcp-server/blob/f678ce9c11f2d9eb02b2c4f56a86c948558b127f/extension/background.js#L37-L55) (arming from the toolbar), [06-F15](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L474-L485) (picking a tab from its context menu), [18-F11](https://github.com/eyalzh/browser-control-mcp/blob/e53f0e50e2778543d2c2b5dadbe565be82920fbf/firefox-extension/options.ts#L443-L502) (one origin at a time).
- PM2 Enforcement. ext/background refuses any tool call on a tab without a matching grant. ext/page checks the origin again just before it acts. References: [06-F04](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/extension/background.js#L724-L729), [07-F20](https://github.com/elys0912/InariMCP/blob/1ac430daf8ca95e0dfbc72b5083ee1302443376f/packages/mcp-server/src/relay/approval.ts#L221-L266), [17-F02](https://github.com/g-lacerda/browser-control-mcp/blob/37fd83486d60ea37122f207b857376fdd8fd7a55/firefox-extension/abas-claude.ts#L10-L28).
- PM3 Navigation. Navigation to another origin suspends the grant until the user approves the new origin.
- PM4 Session tabs. Tabs that Claude opens belong to the session and start with a grant only for the origin the user approved. Reference: [10-F23](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/extension/background/sessions.js#L201-L213).
- PM5 Listing. Tab listing shows granted and session-owned tabs only. Other tabs appear as a count.
- PM6 Host permission. The Firefox host permission is separate from a grant and is requested per origin from a user gesture. `<all_urls>` is optional. Reference: [18-F11](https://github.com/eyalzh/browser-control-mcp/blob/e53f0e50e2778543d2c2b5dadbe565be82920fbf/firefox-extension/options.ts#L443-L502).
- PM7 Approval. Sensitive actions (submit, purchase-like, delete, download, upload, leaving the origin, reading cookies or history) need an approval shown in extension UI, never in page DOM. An approval binds to the tool, the tab, the origin and an argument hash, and it expires. It covers one call, without exception (FC-GRANT-11). Limit: what shows only after a click, such as a script that leaves the origin or a download that the server starts, is stopped afterwards, and the download only with the optional downloads permission (FC-ACT-01). References: [07-F20](https://github.com/elys0912/InariMCP/blob/1ac430daf8ca95e0dfbc72b5083ee1302443376f/packages/mcp-server/src/relay/approval.ts#L221-L266) (binding, expiry, popup outside the page), [01-F17](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/extension/chat.js#L494-L536) (card in the sidebar; its focus behavior must not be copied).
- PM8 Secret fields. Password, one-time-code and payment fields are masked in every read path and refused in every typing and key path. References: [07-F22](https://github.com/elys0912/InariMCP/blob/1ac430daf8ca95e0dfbc72b5083ee1302443376f/packages/shared/src/field-mask.ts#L25-L32), [10-F11](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/host/src/tools.js#L72-L85), [09-F13](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/plugin/server/bridge.js#L786-L791).
- PM9 Stop. A pause switch and a per-tab stop end running work. Source 17 shows the gap to avoid: its pause does not stop a call that is already running.
- PM10 Log. Every decision, refusals included, is logged locally without page content or secrets.

All rows describe the everyday product, unless they name the optional BiDi backend, which is off by default and never touches the everyday profile. Which component holds that backend is decided in the advanced run; the Target column names mcp/server until then.

## Capability matrix

Rules that hold for every row. The names are proposals.

- A call names its tab by `tabId`. Errors are structured replies with a code and a message. Codes: `no_grant`, `read_only_grant`, `grant_suspended`, `origin_changed`, `restricted_page`, `permission_required`, `approval_required`, `approval_denied`, `approval_expired`, `paused`, `stopped`, `conflict`, `stale_ref`, `covered`, `secret_field`, `invalid_argument`, `path_not_allowed`, `too_large`, `timeout`, `not_connected`, `unknown_tool`, and the codes of the table after this list, which rows name in words.
- An argument the tool does not know is refused with `invalid_argument`. It is never ignored, as happened in [12-F17](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/background.js#L659-L720) and [12-F03](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/mcp-server/server.py#L210-L222).
- If several checks fail, the reply holds the first code of this order: `unknown_tool` and the schema check of FC-AGT-03 (`invalid_argument`), `not_connected`, `version_mismatch`, `session_ended`, `settings_unreadable`, `paused`, `conflict`, `no_grant`, `site_blocked`, `restricted_page`, `grant_suspended`, `stopped`, `tool_off`, `read_only_grant`, `permission_required`, `rate_limited`. Then come the row's own checks of values and target, and then the approval. Origin and target are checked again just before the action (FC-ACT-31, FC-GRANT-11). A grant that is suspended only because Firefox took a permission away gives `permission_required` (FC-GRANT-14). `connection_status` answers in each of these states and names it (FC-CONN-08).
- "Level read" or "level act" names the grant level a tool needs (PM1). Where the actions or modes of one tool differ, each has its own level (FC-GRANT-04).
- A session is one mcp/server process or one sidebar chat while its link to one Firefox profile holds, each with its own current tab ([01-F26](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/host/native-host.mjs#L140-L141)). A session that has ended is never resumed (FC-CONN-14). The session folder is a folder of that session in the user's temp directory.
- A tool that needs a Firefox permission the user has not given shows the request in the sidebar and returns `permission_required`, unless its row says what still works without it. A left click without the downloads permission still works: a link with a download attribute still asks, and the reply says that the download guard is off (FC-ACT-01). No tool opens a tab, a window or a prompt to ask for a permission.

| Code | Meaning | Rows that name it in words |
|---|---|---|
| `load_failed` | The page did not load. The message holds Firefox's error | FC-TAB-08, FC-TAB-17, FC-TAB-26 (load error) |
| `no_history` | No entry in that direction of the tab history | FC-TAB-18 (no-entry error) |
| `limit_reached` | The session owns as many tabs as it may | FC-TAB-33 (limit error) |
| `busy` | Another capture did not finish in time | FC-SHOT-01 (busy error) |
| `unknown_request` | The request ID is used up or belongs to another session | FC-TAB-04 (unknown-request error) |
| `expired` | An image ID, or a screenshot whose coordinates are used, is too old | FC-ACT-03 (new screenshot), FC-ACT-24 (expired error) |
| `not_found` | No such tab, container, profile label or image ID for this session | FC-TAB-07, FC-TAB-10, FC-ACT-24, FC-CONN-01 |
| `ambiguous` | Several matches. The reply holds the candidates | FC-READ-08, FC-ACT-12, FC-ACT-13, FC-ACT-14, FC-CONN-01 |
| `no_match` | No option matches. The reply holds the options where the row says so | FC-ACT-13, FC-ACT-14 |
| `not_actionable` | The target is hidden, disabled, already open, without size, or takes no text | FC-ACT-04, FC-ACT-08, FC-ACT-13, FC-ACT-14, FC-ACT-20, FC-SHOT-05 |
| `not_allowed` | A fixed rule of the row forbids it and no approval unlocks it | FC-TAB-25, FC-TAB-29, FC-ACT-01, FC-ACT-10, FC-ACT-12, FC-SHOT-01 (minimized window) |
| `not_supported` | Private windows and their containers | FC-TAB-10, FC-TAB-32 |
| `dialog_open` | A native dialog may be open in the tab | FC-ACT-26 |
| `site_blocked` | A site rule of the user forbids the site, or the address of the tab cannot be read | FC-GRANT-01 |
| `tool_off` | The tool, or this mode of it, is off, because the user turned it off or because it is off on a new install. The message names the switch | FC-DATA-01, FC-DATA-02, FC-DATA-03, FC-DATA-04, FC-DATA-06, FC-DATA-08, FC-DATA-09, FC-DATA-10, FC-DATA-16, FC-DATA-17, FC-DBG-05, FC-GRANT-15 |
| `rate_limited` | Too many calls or waiting approvals in this session. The message holds the waiting time | FC-GRANT-22 |
| `settings_unreadable` | The stored settings cannot be read | FC-GRANT-23 |
| `outcome_unknown` | The link broke after the call was sent. The call is not repeated | FC-CONN-10, FC-CONN-11 |
| `version_mismatch` | Two parts speak different protocol versions. The message names the older one | FC-CONN-18 |
| `cancelled` | The client cancelled the call | FC-CONN-12 |
| `unknown_tool` | No tool of that name, or the chosen tool set hides it. The message suggests an update | FC-CONN-03, FC-DBG-09, FC-AGT-03, FC-AGT-12 |
| `session_ended` | The earlier session of this client has ended. A new one without grants has begun, and this call was not carried out | FC-CONN-10, FC-CONN-31 |

Where a row only says "refused", the code is `invalid_argument` for a value that breaks a limit of the row, `no_grant` for a point over a frame that is not granted (FC-ACT-03), and `permission_required` for a capture without consent (FC-SHOT-03).

### TAB: tabs, windows, groups, containers, navigation

| FC-TAB-01 | List granted tabs | `tabs_context` returns one entry per granted or session-owned tab: tabId, windowId, title, URL without query and fragment (full URL only on request), grant level and state, active, group, container, loading state, last use, and the session's current tab. All other tabs appear only as a number. With `createIfEmpty` and no tab yet, it opens one blank session tab. Several sources list every tab with its URL and title and no grant ([04-F09](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L879-L886), | FC-TAB-02 | Filter and page the tab list | `tabs_context` accepts title text, URL text with `*` wildcards, windowId, active, pinned, audible, limit (default 50, at most 500) and offset. Text matching ignores case. No regular expressions from the agent. Filters apply after the grant filter, so they cannot probe other tabs. In [12-F17](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/background.js#L659-L720) three filters were silently dropped because of an argument-name mapping. | [11-F01](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/tabs.ts#L36-L67) [02-F03](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L167-L222) [12-F19](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/background.js#L766-L804) [12-F17](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/background.js#L659-L720) [18-F03](https://github.com/eyalzh/browser-control-mcp/blob/e53f0e50e2778543d2c2b5dadbe565be82920fbf/mcp-server/server.ts#L50-L89) [20-F04](https://github.com/ICWR-TEAM/Firefox-Browser-MCP/blob/2d475661406eb06788fcc505c39b72e1882c3b90/mcp-server/src/firefox_browser_mcp/server.py#L101-L108) [17-F01](https://github.com/g-lacerda/browser-control-mcp/blob/37fd83486d60ea37122f207b857376fdd8fd7a55/mcp-server/server.ts#L16-L94) | Firefox 160; tabs permission | ext/background, mcp/server `tabs_context` | Grant 60 fixture tabs. `limit: 50` returns 50 entries and nextOffset 50. A URL filter that matches only an ungranted tab returns none. An unknown filter name returns `invalid_argument`. | planned |
| FC-TAB-03 | Details of one tab | `tab_info` returns id, URL, title, load status, active, pinned, muted, audible, discarded, container, window state and focus for one granted tab, from the tab record only, so it also works while the page is busy. The active tab of the window is reported only when it is granted. [02-F13](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L437-L455) returns the raw URL of the active tab with no check. | [12-F18](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/background.js#L723-L763) [11-F09](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/extension/src/content/ax.ts#L2985-L3040) [20-F14](https://github.com/ICWR-TEAM/Firefox-Browser-MCP/blob/2d475661406eb06788fcc505c39b72e1882c3b90/mcp-server/src/firefox_browser_mcp/server.py#L260-L264) [02-F13](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L437-L455) | Firefox 160; tabs permission | ext/background, mcp/server `tab_info` | Make an ungranted tab active and call `tab_info` without a tabId while the session has no current tab. The reply is `invalid_argument` and holds no URL or title. Grant the tab; the same call with its tabId returns both. | planned |
| FC-TAB-04 | Ask the user to pick a tab | `tab_request` takes a reason, returns a requestId at once and shows the request in the sidebar, in the tab context menu and as a toolbar badge. The user picks a tab and a level, or refuses. `tab_request_result` answers at once, or waits as long as the call asks (FC-CONN-12), with pending, granted with the tabId, denied or timed out (the request ends after 120 s). A final answer is returned once, and only to the session that asked. The agent can never take an existing tab by ID, unlike [04-F10](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L887-L914), [06-F07](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L353-L369) and [16-F13](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/commands.ts#L421-L455), where any ID is accepted without the user. | [06-F16](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L487-L498) [06-F15](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L474-L485) [04-F10](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L887-L914) [06-F07](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L353-L369) [16-F13](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/commands.ts#L421-L455) [23-F01](https://github.com/nullthrix/firefox-mcp-server/blob/f678ce9c11f2d9eb02b2c4f56a86c948558b127f/extension/background.js#L37-L55) | Firefox 160; menus and tabs permissions; PM1 | ext/sidebar, ext/grants, mcp/server `tab_request`, `tab_request_result` | Call `tab_request` from session A. Pick tab 1 in its context menu. A poll by A returns tab 1, a second poll and a poll by session B return an unknown-request error. A call on tab 2 by its ID returns `no_grant`. | planned |
| FC-TAB-05 | Release a tab | `tabs_release` ends the session's use of a tab. The grant of this session ends, the overlay disappears, page hooks are removed, and console and network buffers of that tab are cleared at once. In [01-F15](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/server/tools.mjs#L279-L285) capture only stops after the next navigation; here it stops with the call. The tab stays open. | [06-F09](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L384-L394) [01-F15](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/server/tools.mjs#L279-L285) [05-F03](https://github.com/MashdorDev/mcp-bridge-for-firefox/blob/a1cb01b79a17f5b3b1f955180d4e4849137eeb89/server/mcp_tools.py#L926-L1018) [04-F10](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L887-L914) | Firefox 160; no extra permission | ext/grants, ext/capture, ext/overlay, mcp/server `tabs_release` | Grant a fixture page that logs once per second, read its console, release it, wait 3 s, grant it again and read the console. No entry from the released period is present. | planned |
| FC-TAB-06 | One session per tab | A tab granted to one session is refused to every other session with `conflict`, until it is released or the session ends. The reply names no other session's data. In [04-F10](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L887-L914) an attached tab is open to all agents, and in [04-F11](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L915-L928) a tab with an unknown owner can be closed by anyone. | [06-F07](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L353-L369) [06-F09](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L384-L394) [15-F13](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/bridge-server/mcp-server.js#L351-L365) [04-F10](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L887-L914) [04-F11](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L915-L928) [02-F56](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/broker-core.ts#L82) [06-F52](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/native-host/src/index.js#L185-L192) | Firefox 160; no extra permission; PM2 | ext/grants, ext/background | Start two MCP servers. | FC-TAB-07 | Target tab resolution | Tab tools take a numeric tabId. Without it they use the session's current tab, which is the last tab the session opened, navigated or selected. ### READ: outline, text, search, waits for content

| ID | Capability | Observable behavior | Sources | Platform prerequisites | Target | Acceptance test | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-READ-01 | Page outline | `read_page` (level read) returns an indented outline of roles, names, states, link targets and values with `ref_N` handles. Inputs: filter (interactive or all), depth, ref_id for a subtree, viewport only, max_chars (default 50,000). Center coordinates are added on request. When the outline is too long, side content such as footers and long lists is first collapsed to one line with a count and a ref, then the rest is cut at a line end and the full size is given. The walk stops at 20,000 nodes and says so. Hidden elements are left out. Nothing is written into the page DOM. [02-F09](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L330-L374), ### ACT: input, forms, upload, dialogs, waits that belong to actions

All actions need level act unless a row says otherwise, and follow PM2, PM7 and PM8.

| ID | Capability | Observable behavior | Sources | Platform prerequisites | Target | Acceptance test | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-ACT-01 | Left click | `computer` left_click takes a ref, a locator or a coordinate. It scrolls the element into view, then sends pointer and mouse events and exactly one click at its center, and forwards a label click to its checkbox. A click on a link whose scheme is not http or https is refused. An approval comes before the click (PM7) for submit-like, purchase-like and delete-like controls, for a link to another origin and for a link with a download attribute. For a link the card shows the full address. Approving a link to another origin is also the approval that PM3 asks for, for that origin only. What only shows after the click is stopped at that point: a script or redirect that leaves the origin suspends the grant, and a download is cancelled, no file is left and the reply is `approval_required` with the address. The reply says that the click was sent, and a repeat of the call asks first. A download counts as the page's when it starts during the action and its address or referrer has the tab's origin or is the clicked address; no other download is touched. This download guard needs the optional downloads permission. Without it a click still works and a link with a download attribute still asks, but no download is cancelled, and the reply says that the download guard is off. Events are not trusted, and the reply says so when nothing changed. In [12-F04](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/content.js#L608-L642) a quote in the text breaks the call. | | FC-ACT-08 | Type text | type inserts text at the caret of the focused editable element, or of a ref, which it focuses first. Inputs, text areas and rich editors are supported. Frameworks see input events. The reply says how many characters arrived after a read-back and never repeats the text. No focused editable element is an error. An editor that rejects the text is an error. The OS clipboard is not used; [06-F27](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/tools.js#L68-L90) has such a paste mode. | | FC-ACT-13 | Choose options of a native select | By value, then by label. Label matching ignores case, and an exact match comes first: the label exactly as given, then the whole label with other case, then a label part if only one option matches. | FC-ACT-21 | Upload files | `file_upload` attaches named local files to a file input by ref, without a file dialog. mcp/server reads them, and only from the allowed folders: the roots that the MCP client reports plus a list that the user sets in options. The list is empty by default and no tool can change it. Without roots and with an empty list every path is refused. Links are resolved first (symbolic links, junctions and `..`), the file is read from the resolved path, and a resolved path outside the allowed folders returns `path_not_allowed`. Inside them, hidden files, key-like and credential-like names, files with a second hard link (as in [24-F26](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#upload-files-to-web-pages)) and non-files are refused with the same code. Instead of a path, a call may give a file name and up to 500 KB of text. At most 10 files and 10 MB per call (setting up to 50 MB), else `too_large`. An approval shows the full resolved path, the name and the size of each file; for text, the name and the size. Files travel in 512 KB chunks. The ref is identity-checked again just before the files are set; [02-F34](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L1021-L1047) skips that. Captures need level read. They need `<all_urls>`, or activeTab from a user gesture on the visible tab (see "Platform prerequisites").

| FC-SHOT-04 | Zoom into a region | `computer` zoom takes a rectangle in CSS pixels and returns it at the device pixel ratio, or at a given scale from 0.1 to 4. Without it, after the toolbar click of FC-SHOT-03, it uses captureVisibleTab. That returns the visible area of the visible active tab only, so the rectangle is cut out of it, and a scale above the device pixel ratio returns `permission_required`. Any other tab returns `permission_required`. | FC-SHOT-12 | Step through a long page | `scroll_capture` scrolls in steps (default 80 percent of the view, at most 20 steps), and returns the visible interactive elements of each step, with a picture per step on request. The element lists need only the origin's host permission. A picture needs `<all_urls>`, or the toolbar click of FC-SHOT-03 on the visible active tab. Without either, the steps come back without pictures and the reply names the missing permission. | [12-F25](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/extension/content.js#L1275-L1321) | Firefox 160; host permission for the origin; for pictures as FC-SHOT-01 | ext/page, ext/capture, mcp/server `scroll_capture` | Page with a lazy list of 40 rows: the steps together list all rows once, and the scroll position at the end equals the start. It works with level read, and the event log of the page holds no event with `isTrusted: false`. With pictures asked for, but without `<all_urls>` and without a toolbar click: the lists come back, no image does, and the reply names the permission. | planned-adapted: pictures need `<all_urls>` or a toolbar click, the element lists do not |
| FC-SHOT-13 | Save a page as PDF | `save_pdf` saves the active granted tab through Firefox's own function, with orientation and backgrounds. Windows shows a save dialog, and the user picks the place or cancels. That dialog is the user's decision, so no card is shown. The reply says saved, replaced, cancelled or failed. It does not know the path. If the dialog is still open after 120 s, the reply is `timeout` and says so. An inactive tab needs `activate: true`. [13-F12](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/print.ts#L6-L30) says an extension cannot do this; tabs.saveAsPDF can. | [13-F12](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/print.ts#L6-L30) | Firefox 160; tabs.saveAsPDF (81, opens a save dialog) | ext/capture, mcp/server `save_pdf` | Call it on a granted tab, choose a place: a PDF with the page text exists and the reply is saved. Cancel: the reply is cancelled and there is no file. | planned-adapted: the user chooses the file in a Windows dialog, no silent saving |
| FC-SHOT-14 | GIF recording | `gif_creator` starts, stops, exports and clears a recording of one granted tab. Starting needs an approval and is one call (FC-GRANT-11), and the overlay shows that recording is on. A frame is stored before and after each action, at most 200 frames. Export adds click marks, drag paths, labels, a progress bar and a mark (each can be turned off), quality 1 to 30. Frames are covered as in FC-SHOT-08. Saving goes through a save dialog or the session folder, with a note to review it before sharing. Recording stops when the grant is suspended or ends, and on a pause. | [24-F43](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) [24-F18](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#record-a-demo-gif) [24-F12](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#browser-tools-in-plan-mode) | Firefox 160; as FC-SHOT-02; canvas and a bundled GIF encoder (P2); PM7 | ext/capture, ext/overlay, mcp/server `gif_creator` | Record three clicks and export: a playable GIF with at least four frames and three click marks. Navigate to another origin during recording: it stops and says why. Deny the start: no frame exists. | planned-adapted: frames come from tab captures around actions, encoded in the extension |
| FC-SHOT-15 | Video recording of a tab | Not in the everyday product. In the advanced run, an optional BiDi backend may record video in an isolated profile (FC-CONN-28). [13-F23](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/screencast.ts#L31-L64) records through the remote agent to a path that Firefox chooses. FC-SHOT-14 is the replacement in the everyday product. | [13-F23](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/screencast.ts#L31-L64) | Firefox 160; D6, D7 | mcp/server (optional BiDi backend) | The everyday tool list has no video tool, and `gif_creator` with `format: video` returns `invalid_argument`. Isolated profile (advanced run): a recording of a fixture is a playable file. | deferred: the only source way needs WebDriver BiDi, so it waits for the optional BiDi backend of the advanced run |

Regular expressions, as in the benchmark, are not taken. clear is a choice. Entries are page data and marked as untrusted. | FC-DBG-05 | Read request and response bodies | Off at first: until the user turns response bodies on, a call that asks for bodies returns `tool_off` (FC-GRANT-15). `bodies: start` starts a capture in one granted tab. That call asks (PM7) and is one call. The capture ends on `bodies: stop`, on a move to another origin, on a release or any other end of the grant, on a pause, or after 10 minutes, and the next start asks again. While it runs, text bodies (JSON, XML, text, script) up to 512 KB each and 20 MB per tab are kept, and secret-like fields are redacted in both directions. Kept bodies can be read without a further card, and are deleted on a new origin, on a release and at the end of the grant. Binary bodies are only measured. [03-F16](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_extra.py#L70-L166) promises redaction of responses and does not do it, [05-F23](https://github.com/MashdorDev/mcp-bridge-for-firefox/blob/a1cb01b79a17f5b3b1f955180d4e4849137eeb89/server/mcp_tools.py#L3701-L3910) records request bodies whatever its switch says, and [14-F29](https://github.com/ThinkerYzu/foxmcp/blob/cc97d6ae90a9cd2a7bdeb9e38feaf9d29c395921/server/mcp_tools.py#L2891-L3005) does not limit request bodies. The stream filter passes every chunk on unchanged ([16-F21](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/commands.ts#L610-L646), [02-F37](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L1127-L1207)). | FC-DBG-07 | Page script cannot forge entries | Console entries reach the extension only through functions that the content script exports into the page and that keep no reference on the page's window. | FC-DBG-12 | Firefox internals, preferences, other add-ons and process output | Not possible in the everyday extension. A WebExtension cannot reach the browser window's code, the preferences or the output of the Firefox process, and cannot install other add-ons from a path. Companion does not list them either. The user changes these by hand. [03-F18](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_webdriver.py#L195-L299) runs privileged code and sets any preference with no check in code, [13-F27](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/bidi.ts#L8-L39) sends nearly any remote-agent command, and [13-F16](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/firefox-management.ts#L19-L43) prints environment values. In the advanced run, an optional BiDi backend may offer these in an isolated profile (FC-CONN-28). Not all of it is plain BiDi: [13-F25](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/firefox-prefs.ts#L41-L62) and [13-F26](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/privileged-context.ts#L13-L24) need a chrome context with system access allowed, and [13-F16](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/firefox-management.ts#L19-L43) reads the output of a Firefox that its server started. | [13-F25](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/firefox-prefs.ts#L41-L62) [13-F26](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/privileged-context.ts#L13-L24) [13-F27](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/bidi.ts#L8-L39) [13-F24](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/webextension.ts#L31-L63) [13-F16](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/firefox-management.ts#L19-L43) [03-F18](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_webdriver.py#L195-L299) | Firefox 160; D6, D7. The sources need the remote agent | mcp/server (optional BiDi backend) | The manifest has no experiment API and no management permission, and the everyday tool list has no such tool. Isolated profile (advanced run): a preference set there is unchanged in the everyday profile. | deferred: optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile in the advanced run; a WebExtension cannot do it, and privileged access needs its own safety decision |
| FC-DBG-13 | Script sources, breakpoints and logpoints | Not possible from a WebExtension, and the everyday extension asks for no debugger permission. The user opens Firefox's own developer tools. FC-DBG-01 and FC-DBG-02 give console output and errors. In the advanced run, an optional BiDi backend lists scripts, reads their sources and sets logpoints in an isolated profile (FC-CONN-28). [13-F20](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/debugging.ts#L29-L37) and [13-F21](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/debugging.ts#L65-L84) do this with moz:debugging BiDi commands and check the Firefox version first. | [13-F20](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/debugging.ts#L29-L37) [13-F21](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/debugging.ts#L65-L84) | Firefox 160; D7 | mcp/server (optional BiDi backend) | The everyday tool list has no debugger tool, and the manifest has no debugger and no devtools entry. Isolated profile (advanced run): list the scripts of a fixture, set a logpoint on one line and run the line twice. Two results come back, and after removal none. | deferred: Firefox-native debugging through the optional BiDi backend in the advanced run; Firefox has no debugger permission for extensions |
| FC-DBG-14 | Performance profiler | Not possible from a WebExtension. FC-DBG-15 gives page timing. [13-F40](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/plugins/firefox-mcp/skills/web-performance/SKILL.md#L1-L61) and [13-F41](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/plugins/firefox-mcp/skills/web-performance/references/analysis-playbook.md#L1-L62) are written guidance around the same remote-agent commands. In the advanced run, an optional BiDi backend records profiles in an isolated profile (FC-CONN-28). [13-F22](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/profiler.ts#L33-L43) does this with moz:profiler BiDi commands. | [13-F22](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/profiler.ts#L33-L43) [13-F40](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/plugins/firefox-mcp/skills/web-performance/SKILL.md#L1-L61) [13-F41](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/plugins/firefox-mcp/skills/web-performance/references/analysis-playbook.md#L1-L62) | Firefox 160; D6, D7 | mcp/server (optional BiDi backend) | The everyday tool list has no profiler tool, and `page_timing` with `profile: true` returns `invalid_argument`. Isolated profile (advanced run): start and stop a profile around a fixture with a 2 s script. A profile file is saved, and the reply names it. | deferred: optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile in the advanced run; the sources use profiler commands of the remote agent |
| FC-DBG-15 | Page timing | `page_timing` returns navigation timing, first and largest paint where Firefox reports them, long resource loads, and the marks and measures of the page, for a granted tab. Entry types that Firefox does not support are named as missing. Addresses are redacted as in FC-GRANT-20. | [13-F41](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/plugins/firefox-mcp/skills/web-performance/references/analysis-playbook.md#L1-L62) [13-F22](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/profiler.ts#L33-L43) | Firefox 160; Performance API in a content script | ext/page, mcp/server `page_timing` | Fixture with a 2 s image and one user mark. The reply lists the image with at least 2,000 ms, the mark, and the entry types that Firefox lacks. | planned-adapted: the Performance API of the page instead of the profiler |

### DATA: cookies, storage, history, bookmarks, downloads, fetch, sessions, clipboard

The cookies, storage, history, bookmarks and clipboard groups are off on a new install (FC-GRANT-15). A test of a row in these groups turns the group on first, after the step that expects `tool_off`.

| ID | Capability | Observable behavior | Sources | Platform prerequisites | Target | Acceptance test | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-DATA-01 | Read cookies of a granted origin | `get_cookies` lists the cookies that the granted tab's origin and container can see: name, domain, path, flags, expiry. Off at first: until the user turns the cookies group on, a call returns `tool_off` (FC-GRANT-15). Each call needs an approval (PM7) that names the site. Values show as a mask. With `values: true` the card lists the cookie names, and only those values come back, marked as secret. Values never reach the log. A call without a tab, or for another domain, is refused. [02-F38](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L1209-L1242) skips its site check when no URL is given, [21-F22](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L307-L317) reads any domain, and [11-F20](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L139-L193) and [19-F12](https://github.com/sinamohsenifar/foxbridge-mcp/blob/b46603599a437f4918eae0256fecb78ab2576141/src/index.ts#L226-L228) return values without asking. | [11-F20](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L139-L193) [02-F38](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L1209-L1242) [19-F12](https://github.com/sinamohsenifar/foxbridge-mcp/blob/b46603599a437f4918eae0256fecb78ab2576141/src/index.ts#L226-L228) [21-F22](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L307-L317) | Firefox 160; optional cookies permission; host permission for the origin; PM7 | ext/background, ext/grants, ext/sidebar, mcp/server `get_cookies` | Fixture with one script-readable and one httpOnly cookie. On a new install: `tool_off` and no card. Turn the group on. Deny: `approval_denied` and no name. Approve: two names, both values masked. `values: true`, approved: two values, and neither is in the log file. A domain argument for another site returns `invalid_argument`. | planned |
| FC-DATA-02 | Set or delete a cookie | `set_cookie` and `remove_cookie` change one cookie of the granted origin. Off at first, with the cookies group (FC-GRANT-15). The card shows the site, the name and, for a set, the value. Domain and path must match the origin. httpOnly cookies are not changed. In [21-F22](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L307-L317) any cookie of any site can be changed, and in [11-F20](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L139-L193) the agent itself supplies the confirm flag. | [11-F20](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L139-L193) [21-F22](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L307-L317) [19-F19](https://github.com/sinamohsenifar/foxbridge-mcp/blob/b46603599a437f4918eae0256fecb78ab2576141/src/index.ts#L121-L132) | Firefox 160; optional cookies permission; host permission for the origin; PM7 | ext/background, ext/grants, ext/sidebar, mcp/server `set_cookie`, `remove_cookie` | Set a cookie and approve: the fixture page reads it. Remove it: gone. Remove the httpOnly session cookie: `not_allowed` and the user is still signed in. A domain of another site returns `invalid_argument`, with no card. | planned |
| FC-DATA-03 | Read page storage | `page_storage` lists or reads localStorage and sessionStorage of the granted tab's top frame, after an approval as in FC-DATA-01. Off at first: until the user turns the storage group on, a call returns `tool_off` (FC-GRANT-15). Values longer than 20,000 characters are cut. Values of keys with credential-like names, and values that look like tokens, show as a mask. [11-F17](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L195-L240) and [21-F23](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L83-L113) return everything unfiltered. | [11-F17](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L195-L240) [21-F23](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L83-L113) | Firefox 160; content script; PM7; FC-GRANT-20 | ext/page, ext/grants, ext/sidebar, mcp/server `page_storage` | Storage with the keys `theme` and `access_token`. On a new install: `tool_off` and no card. Turn the group on. Deny: no key comes back. Approve: `theme` with its value, `access_token` with the mask. | planned |
| FC-DATA-04 | Change page storage | `page_storage` with set, delete or clear changes the granted origin's storage, level act, with an approval that shows the key and the value or, for clear, the number of keys. Off at first, with the storage group (FC-GRANT-15). [19-F19](https://github.com/sinamohsenifar/foxbridge-mcp/blob/b46603599a437f4918eae0256fecb78ab2576141/src/index.ts#L121-L132) clears storage of the active origin of a separate browser. | [11-F17](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/tools/browser.ts#L195-L240) [21-F23](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/browser-api.js#L83-L113) [19-F19](https://github.com/sinamohsenifar/foxbridge-mcp/blob/b46603599a437f4918eae0256fecb78ab2576141/src/index.ts#L121-L132) | Firefox 160; content script; PM7 | ext/page, ext/grants, ext/sidebar, mcp/server `page_storage` | Set `theme` and approve: the page reads the new value. ### GRANT: grants, approvals, rules, redaction, log, pause

Where the security defects found in the sources are tested:

- Origin checks, bind address and missing authentication: FC-CONN-05, FC-CONN-06 and FC-CONN-20.
- Unauthenticated status routes: FC-CONN-07 and FC-CONN-08.
- Pipe and key-file access lists, key exposure: FC-CONN-04 and FC-CONN-19.
- Takeover of a connection or a session: FC-CONN-14 and FC-CONN-16.
- Approvals that a page, a client or another extension can forge: FC-GRANT-09, FC-GRANT-10, FC-GRANT-11 and FC-GRANT-17.
- Logs and captured entries that a page can forge: FC-GRANT-19 and FC-DBG-07.
- Secrets in logs and replies: FC-GRANT-18, FC-GRANT-20 and FC-CONN-29.
- Passwords that reads return or typing accepts: FC-GRANT-21, with FC-READ-06, FC-ACT-11 and FC-SHOT-08.
- Tools without a check, and lists that leave tools out: FC-GRANT-01, FC-GRANT-04 and FC-GRANT-15.
- Hidden test actions: FC-CONN-22 and FC-INST-09.
- Settings that an agent, an import or a broken file loosens: FC-GRANT-23.
- Stated limits: another program of the same user can read the pipe key (FC-CONN-04), redaction works by names and token shapes (FC-GRANT-20), hidden instructions can mislead Claude inside its grants (FC-GRANT-25), secret fields are found by a heuristic (FC-GRANT-21), and without the optional downloads permission a download that the server starts after a click is not stopped (FC-ACT-01).

| FC-GRANT-01 | Site rules for reads | The user can list sites that are never read or acted on, sites that ask before each read, and an optional allow list. The rules are checked against the real URL of the tab and of each frame at the time of the call. A tab whose address cannot be read is refused. They apply to every tool, tab lists included. An entry covers its subdomains, the block list wins over the allow list, and a change takes effect at the next check. | FC-GRANT-02 | Only a user gesture makes a grant | A grant starts from the toolbar button, the tab context menu or the sidebar, and names one tab, one origin and one level (PM1). No MCP tool, page script, message from a content script, settings file, client approval dialog or command line can create, widen or extend one. A tab that the session opens starts with a grant under PM4 only (FC-TAB-08, FC-TAB-09, FC-ACT-05): for an origin that the user approved in a card, or that an active grant of this session covers, and at no higher level. | FC-CONN-05 | Both sides prove the key | After connecting, mcp/server and host each send a fresh nonce and answer with an HMAC over both nonces, the role and the sender's own protocol version. The host answers only after it has checked the client's answer (FC-CONN-07). No command is read before both answers are checked in constant time, and only then are the versions compared (FC-CONN-18). The key never crosses the pipe. There is no second, older login. In [10-F27](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/host/src/native-host.js#L69-L78) only the host proves the key, [06-F02](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/native-host/src/socket-server.js#L22-L37) and | FC-CONN-10 | Reconnect | After a drop the extension reopens the native port after 1 s, doubling to 30 s, without end, and mcp/server reconnects on its next call. A new call while the link is down returns `not_connected` with the next step. Calls in flight end at once with `outcome_unknown`, and so do queued calls, which are not kept. A lost link ends the session and its grants (FC-CONN-15). The first call after the reconnect, other than `connection_status`, starts a new session without grants, is not carried out, and returns `session_ended`. The sidebar shows the state, has Retry now, and offers one button that grants the same tabs again, which is a user gesture (PM1). The button names the tabs, origins and levels and the new session that gets them. It leaves out tabs that are closed or on another origin by then, and sites of FC-GRANT-13, and it is gone after a restart of Firefox (FC-GRANT-06). In [04-F33](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/extension/background.js#L47-L57) the extension gives up after 10 attempts, and so does that of | FC-CONN-12 | Timeouts and cancellation | Every tool has a time limit in one table, 5 s to 120 s, shown in its description. Only tools that wait for the user may take longer, at most 300 s in all: `request_user`, `tab_request_result`, and calls with a pending approval, also as steps of a batch (FC-AGT-05). The time that an approval is pending does not count against the tool's own limit. The limit the client asks for is kept up to the maximum. A timeout or a cancel notice stops the work in the page and returns `timeout` or `cancelled` with what was done so far. In [21-F17](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/channel/server.mjs#L332-L334) the client's limit has no effect, and in [21-F16](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/background.js#L263-L317) the code runs on. | [11-F41](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/bridge/bridge.ts#L88-L91) [02-F66](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/timeouts.ts#L9) [01-F36](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/server/tools.mjs#L47) [23-F14](https://github.com/nullthrix/firefox-mcp-server/blob/f678ce9c11f2d9eb02b2c4f56a86c948558b127f/src/context.ts#L29-L38) [06-F03](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/mcp-server/src/bridge-client.js#L155-L171) [15-F22](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/bridge-server/mcp-server.js#L146-L163) [15-F34](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/bridge-server/server.js#L76-L76) [16-F34](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/engine.ts#L15-L15) [21-F16](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/extension/background/background.js#L263-L317) [21-F17](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/channel/server.mjs#L332-L334) | Firefox 160; no extra permission | shared/protocol, mcp/server, ext/background, ext/page | Cancel a `wait_for` and a slow typing call: both return `cancelled` within 1 s and no character arrives afterwards. | FC-CONN-13 | Message size limits | A message to the extension is at most 1 MB, so longer inputs go in 512 KB chunks with a count and a hash. Replies to the host are at most 8 MB, and one that would be larger is cut or returns `too_large` before it is sent. Before login a connection may send 8 KB. JSON is parsed inside a guard. [18-F14](https://github.com/eyalzh/browser-control-mcp/blob/e53f0e50e2778543d2c2b5dadbe565be82920fbf/mcp-server/browser-api.ts#L48-L70) parses frames without one. | [09-F23](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/plugin/server/bridge.js#L105-L108) [11-F41](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/mcp-server/src/bridge/bridge.ts#L88-L91) [01-F36](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/server/tools.mjs#L47) [18-F14](https://github.com/eyalzh/browser-control-mcp/blob/e53f0e50e2778543d2c2b5dadbe565be82920fbf/mcp-server/browser-api.ts#L48-L70) | Firefox 160; P12 | shared/protocol, host/native, mcp/server, ext/background | Upload 3 MB: it arrives with the same hash. A page outline of 20 MB is cut with the full size. A frame that is no JSON, and one of 2 GB by its length field: the connection closes and the host goes on for others. | planned |
| FC-CONN-14 | Several sessions at once | Each mcp/server process and each chat, while its link holds, is a session with its own ID from the host, name, current tab, grants, approvals and limits. Replies, events, dialog reports and approvals reach only their session. The client cannot choose or reuse an ID, and nobody can resume a session that has ended: after a lost link the host gives a new ID without grants, and only the user's click gives grants again (FC-CONN-10). The mcp/server process of a chat run joins the session of its chat, because the host started that run itself and checks a value that works once. The name comes from the client and can be changed; it shows in the sidebar, on the pill and as the group title. [12-F52](https://github.com/nanogenomic/ClaudeCodeBrowser/blob/96a7fd398f080f30caf34ce4669bee354f1f297f/mcp-server/server.py#L949-L957) has one queue that any host may take from, session ID ([Permission boundaries of 15](audit/15-open-browser-control.md#permission-boundaries)), and in [03-F23](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/bridge.py#L220-L226) clients without a session header share one session. | | FC-CONN-15 | End of a session | When the client exits, its pipe closes or the link to Firefox is lost, its session ends: its grants end, waits and approvals are cancelled, hooks and overlays go, and its tabs stay open, which is the default of FC-TAB-21. An ended session is never resumed (FC-CONN-14). The session of a chat lasts over its runs and ends when the chat is deleted or the link is lost. Tabs that a tool opened for one call are closed by that call. Silence does not end a session; [04-F34](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L83-L86) closes the tabs of an agent after ten minutes. | [06-F02](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/native-host/src/socket-server.js#L22-L37) [10-F23](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/extension/background/sessions.js#L201-L213) [10-F24](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/extension/background/sessions.js#L261-L290) [06-F59](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/firefox-bridge-bot/index.js#L72-L91) [04-F34](https://github.com/boot-industries/claudezilla/blob/5e4a23795d5813224b6eda038cc035d7afc8e508/mcp/server.js#L83-L86) [21-F50](https://github.com/korchasa/foxcode/blob/6c5d0e74b956ee413ed83f4b4f2a637fad080a9f/foxcode/channel/server.mjs#L349-L371) | Firefox 160; tabs permission | host/native, ext/background, ext/overlay | Kill a client during a wait and with a card open: within 2 s the card is gone, the grant list has no entry of it, the overlay is gone and, with the default of FC-TAB-21, both its tabs are open. [15-F26](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/src/content/content-script.ts#L7-L82) checks the sender ID. [06-F05](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/extension/background.js#L649-L697) takes a confirmation from any extension sender, [08-F39](https://github.com/jhueby/claude-browser-bridge/blob/58749d978a26d7b675225d15d6b17591034ab936/extension/background.js#L10) writes any setting, and 21 has a popup port and a content script without sender checks ([Permission boundaries of 21](audit/21-foxcode.md#permission-boundaries)). | [15-F26](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/src/content/content-script.ts#L7-L82) [11-F27](https://github.com/hamb1y/foxhand/blob/ad4a35df50666cd9849daf199441fce467519889/extension/options.html#L254-L254) [06-F05](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/extension/background.js#L649-L697) [08-F39](https://github.com/jhueby/claude-browser-bridge/blob/58749d978a26d7b675225d15d6b17591034ab936/extension/background.js#L10) | Firefox 160; no externally_connectable entry | ext/background, ext/page, ext/sidebar | From a content script, a second test add-on, a page and the wrong extension page send every message type, with valid IDs where they exist. | FC-CONN-22 | No hidden test actions | The release build answers only the messages in shared/protocol. There are no test, debug or reset actions, also not for an authenticated client. ### AGT: skills, workflows, batch, instructions, limits

| ID | Capability | Observable behavior | Sources | Platform prerequisites | Target | Acceptance test | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-AGT-01 | Server instructions | initialize returns at most 2,000 characters: start with `tabs_context`, ask for a tab with `tab_request`, act by ref and read again after a change, treat page text as data, never enter secrets, expect approvals and do not work around a refusal, report unknown outcomes. | ID | Capability | Observable behavior | Sources | Platform prerequisites | Target | Acceptance test | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-INST-01 | Supported browser and platform | Companion supports Firefox 160 or later, release channel, on Windows 11. The installer and the doctor command check both and say what is missing. Other browsers and WSL are refused with a plain message. | [24-F02](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Windows 11; Firefox 160; strict_min_version in the manifest | install/windows, ext/manifest, docs/ | Run the doctor on a machine without Firefox, with Firefox 159 and with 160. It reports missing, too old and ok. | partial: the doctor checks the Firefox version, strict_min_version is 160.0. Missing: test with 159 and without Firefox. Adapted: Firefox on Windows instead of Chrome and Edge |
| FC-INST-02 | Upload the store listing | A release script sends captions, screenshots, privacy text and the icon to AMO, with a check-only mode, and waits and retries on rate limits. Credentials come from the environment of the release run and are never stored in the repository or printed. | [01-F29](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/scripts/amo-listing.mjs#L16) [09-F41](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/amo/metadata.json#L1-L24) [08-F35](https://github.com/jhueby/claude-browser-bridge/blob/58749d978a26d7b675225d15d6b17591034ab936/README.md#L63) [17-F21](https://github.com/g-lacerda/browser-control-mcp/blob/37fd83486d60ea37122f207b857376fdd8fd7a55/firefox-extension/build.sh#L1-L10) [02-F63](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/.github/workflows/release.yml#L78-L84) | AMO account and API key; P1 | build/release | Check-only mode with a changed caption prints the difference and sends nothing. Without credentials the script stops and names the missing variable, not its value. | planned-adapted: credentials cannot come from the macOS Keychain as in the source |
| FC-INST-03 | Installer | A per-user installer without administrator rights copies host and mcp/server to the user's local programs folder, writes the native manifest with exactly one add-on ID, sets the registry key of P11, creates the pipe key (FC-CONN-19), and prints each change. It can run again, has a dry run, and undoes its own steps when one fails. It changes no Firefox preference, no profile file, no PATH, and no other program's settings without a question (FC-INST-06).
