Each has a code place and a sentence in the docs. Sources 03 and 19 declare permissions they do not use ([03](audit/03-agent-f.md#permission-boundaries), [19](audit/19-foxbridge-mcp.md#permission-boundaries)). Which of them Firefox 160 accepts as optional must be checked. | [15-F27](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/src/firefox/manifest.json#L12-L21) [01-F28](https://github.com/thedankest/ccfox/blob/b83591afc331fabcdae5918016c0fd20f9016151/scripts/sign-amo.mjs#L2-L13) [10-F29](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/package.json#L15-L19) [18-F11](https://github.com/eyalzh/browser-control-mcp/blob/e53f0e50e2778543d2c2b5dadbe565be82920fbf/firefox-extension/options.ts#L443-L502) | Firefox 160; permissions.request (55); PM6 | ext/manifest, ext/options, test/ | A test compares the manifest with the list and with the API calls in the code: no unused and no undeclared permission. A row without a test name fails the check. | planned |

## Features that need a different Firefox implementation

The Firefox fit in the findings judges the upstream implementation. This table says what replaces it. Three upstream judgments do not hold for Companion: [13-F12](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/print.ts#L6-L30) (no PDF), since tabs.saveAsPDF exists; [13-F06](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/input.ts#L52-L72) (no file input without a user gesture), since [10-F14](https://github.com/llyfn/claude-in-firefox/blob/989e483d3514c4ff407584cd8952e580af617c1d/host/src/tools.js#L136-L139), [16-F10](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/commands.ts#L312-L337) and [03-F12](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_extra.py#L16-L17) set file inputs from a content script; and [13-F08](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/network.ts#L26-L99) (no response bodies), since webRequest.filterResponseData exists and [16-F21](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/src/server/commands.ts#L610-L646) uses it.

| Topic | Upstream mechanism | Why it does not fit | Firefox Companion replacement | FC IDs | Finding IDs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CDP and the debugger permission | chrome.debugger attaches to a tab for input, captures, response bodies and scripts | Firefox has no debugger permission and no chrome.debugger (version floors, D7) | Content scripts, tabs.captureTab, webRequest and filterResponseData; script debugging waits for the optional BiDi backend (D7) | FC-ACT-01 FC-SHOT-01 FC-DBG-04 FC-DBG-05 FC-DBG-13 FC-INST-16 | [15-F28](https://github.com/smankoo/open-browser-control/blob/f82b6afad22b483950a6013de375422346828027/src/manifest.json#L6-L14) [02-F41](https://github.com/balakumardev/foxpilot/blob/7d9f08ef8da0c3fd89f454b2be4e7c59939c642e/mcp-server/server.ts#L1374-L1412) |
| Marionette and WebDriver BiDi | geckodriver or a remote-debugging port drives input, network, console, viewport, print, recording | Needs launch flags, marks the profile, and opens a port that any local program can use (D6) | WebExtension APIs where they exist; the rest waits for the optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile (advanced run, D7) | FC-CONN-28 FC-ACT-36 FC-DBG-01 FC-DBG-04 FC-TAB-31 FC-SHOT-13 FC-SHOT-15 | [13-F30](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/index.ts#L80-L165) [03-F19](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_webdriver.py#L35-L76) [13-F08](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/network.ts#L26-L99) [13-F10](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/console.ts#L17-L65) [13-F15](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/utilities.ts#L60-L80) [13-F12](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/print.ts#L6-L30) [13-F23](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/screencast.ts#L31-L64) [05-F40](https://github.com/MashdorDev/mcp-bridge-for-firefox/blob/a1cb01b79a17f5b3b1f955180d4e4849137eeb89/server/bidi.py#L1-L237) [13-F32](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/index.ts#L35-L40) |
| Firefox internals, debugger and profiler over the remote agent | Privileged commands read and set preferences, run code in the browser window, list scripts, set logpoints, record profiles | A signed WebExtension has no such access (P1, D6) | Page timing, console output and errors in the everyday extension; the rest waits for the optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile (advanced run, D7) | FC-DBG-12 FC-DBG-13 FC-DBG-14 FC-DBG-15 | [13-F20](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/debugging.ts#L29-L37) [13-F21](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/debugging.ts#L65-L84) [13-F22](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/profiler.ts#L33-L43) [13-F25](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/firefox-prefs.ts#L41-L62) [13-F26](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/privileged-context.ts#L13-L24) [13-F27](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/bidi.ts#L8-L39) [03-F18](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_webdriver.py#L195-L299) |

## Excluded and blocked capabilities

Excluded means a decision of this project. Platform-blocked means that a signed WebExtension on release Firefox cannot do it. Deferred rows are listed after them, because the user meets the same absence in the first release.

| Capability | FC ID | Status | Reason | What the user sees instead |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Drive a separate or headless browser | FC-TAB-35 | excluded | A separate or headless browser is not the signed-in profile; the isolated profile of the optional BiDi backend is FC-CONN-28 | Background tabs of the normal profile (FC-TAB-11, FC-TAB-39) |
| Wait on an agent script | FC-READ-20 | excluded | Agent script in the page | Fixed wait conditions (FC-READ-18) |
| Answer a native dialog that is already open | FC-ACT-26 | platform-blocked | Native dialogs cannot be read or answered | An error within 5 s that asks the user to close the dialog |
| Hold a page dialog until the agent decides | FC-ACT-27 | excluded | Needs a local port and a secret in page scripts | Safe answers during an action, with a report (FC-ACT-25) |
| Run agent-supplied page JavaScript | FC-DBG-09 | excluded | Code supplied at run time conflicts with P2 and P4 | Typed page queries (FC-DBG-10) |
| Send requests with the user's session to any site | FC-DATA-14 | excluded | Would send the user's session to any site | Same-origin reads (FC-DATA-13), opening the page |
| Read or delete profile files on disk | FC-DATA-18 | excluded | Passes by grants and permissions | The approved data tools of the DATA area |
| Open or load local files in a page | FC-DATA-19 | excluded | Needs a local port or file addresses | Uploads (FC-ACT-21) |
| Act on every site without asking | FC-GRANT-08 | excluded | Replaces the user's grant | Grants per tab and origin, approvals for sensitive calls |
| Local HTTP service for clients | FC-CONN-02 | excluded | A port is not needed | stdio and the named pipe |
| Remote access | FC-CONN-25 | excluded | The user must be at the computer | Nothing; remote use is not offered |
| Account match between extension and Claude Code | FC-CONN-26 | excluded | No account is involved | The same Windows user for both ends |
| Start, restart or quit Firefox | FC-CONN-27 | excluded | The browser belongs to the user | `not_connected` with the request to start Firefox |
| Announcements | FC-UI-17 | excluded | The extension contacts no server | Release notes on the store page |
| Unsigned installation | FC-INST-11 | platform-blocked | Release Firefox installs only signed add-ons | The signed add-on |
| Other systems and containers | FC-INST-15 | excluded | Windows 11 only | A plain message of the installer |
| Chrome and Chromium builds | FC-INST-16 | excluded | Firefox only | Nothing; the benchmark covers Chrome |
| Start at logon | FC-INST-18 | excluded | Nothing runs without Firefox or a client | Firefox starts the host |
| Usage counts and error reports | FC-INST-20 | excluded | The extension contacts no server | A local log |

| Capability | FC ID | Deferred because | What the user sees in the first release |
|---|---|---|---|
| Private windows | FC-TAB-32 | Private page data needs its own consent design | Private windows are refused with an explanation |
| Create, change or remove a container | FC-TAB-38 | Lasting change to the profile | Existing containers can be used (FC-TAB-10, FC-TAB-37) |
| Real Windows input through a helper | FC-ACT-35 | Needs its own safety review | Synthetic input |
| Paced input | FC-ACT-37 | Display option without effect | Direct input |
| Trusted input through remote debugging | FC-ACT-36 | Optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile, advanced run (D7) | Synthetic input, and a reply that says when nothing changed |
| Video recording of a tab | FC-SHOT-15 | Optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile, advanced run (D7) | GIF recording (FC-SHOT-14) |
| Firefox internals, preferences, other add-ons and process output | FC-DBG-12 | Optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile, advanced run (D7) | The user changes them in Firefox |
| Script sources, breakpoints and logpoints | FC-DBG-13 | Optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile, advanced run (D7) | Console and errors (FC-DBG-01, FC-DBG-02), Firefox's own tools |
| Performance profiler | FC-DBG-14 | Optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile, advanced run (D7) | Page timing (FC-DBG-15) |
| Attach through the remote agent | FC-CONN-28 | Optional BiDi backend with an isolated profile, advanced run (D7) | WebExtension APIs |
| Override location or user agent | FC-DBG-06 | Changes what sites see | The real location and user agent |
| Script tool in a developer build | FC-DBG-11 | Only in an unsigned developer build | Typed page queries (FC-DBG-10) |
| Notes kept by the agent | FC-DATA-05 | Page data would stay in the profile | Notes of the client or the chat |
| Delete history entries | FC-DATA-07 | Irreversible change outside granted tabs | The user deletes history in Firefox |
| Rules set by an organization | FC-GRANT-26 | Policy delivery was not verified | Options of the user only |
| Work that outlives Firefox | FC-CONN-24 | Needs its own review | Chat runs end with Firefox and can be resumed |
| Scheduled and background tasks | FC-AGT-11 | The user must be present | Work in background tabs during a chat or client session |
| Tools offered by a page | FC-AGT-15 | Page text would become tool descriptions | Pages cannot add tools |
| Other packages of the server | FC-INST-14 | The host needs the installer anyway | One installer and one add-on file |

No source registers MCP prompts or useful MCP resources, so this document plans none. Screenshots can come back as a resource link (FC-SHOT-09).

## Claude in Chrome benchmark coverage

Coverage: full means the same result for the user. Partial means a part is missing. Different means the same need is met in another way. None means it is not offered. Source 24 was audited from documents and tool schemas only; no tool was called. Nothing is implemented yet, so coverage describes the plan.

| Finding | Benchmark capability | FC IDs | Coverage | Gap and reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [24-F01](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#prerequisites) | Sign-in and plan requirements | FC-AGT-16 | different | The user's own Claude Code answers for sign-in and plan. The extension checks neither, because it holds no account (D4). |
| [24-F02](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Browsers and platforms | FC-INST-01 FC-INST-16 | different | Firefox on Windows 11 instead of Chrome and Edge. Other systems are excluded (FC-INST-15). |
| [24-F03](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#install-the-extension-when-claude-asks) | Extension install prompt | FC-INST-10 | different | Claude Code shows no prompt for this add-on. The first call without it returns the steps, and Firefox asks. |
| [24-F04](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#extension-not-detected) | Native messaging host setup | FC-INST-03 FC-INST-07 FC-CONN-09 | different | Companion's installer registers the Firefox host. The sources describe host registration for Chromium browsers only. |
| [24-F05](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#get-started-in-the-cli) | Connection commands | FC-CONN-30 FC-INST-06 FC-CONN-08 | different | Claude Code's MCP commands and the sidebar do the same. The `--chrome` flag belongs to the built-in integration. |
| [24-F06](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#enable-chrome-by-default) | Enabled by default | FC-CONN-30 FC-AGT-12 | different | Added once for the user in Claude Code's MCP settings instead of a browser setting. Tool sets limit the context use. |
| [24-F07](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#get-started-in-the-cli) | Choose among several browsers | FC-CONN-01 FC-CONN-17 | partial | Only Firefox profiles of the same Windows user. Other computers are not reachable (D2, D4). |
| [24-F08](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#connection-drops-during-long-sessions) | Connection status and reconnect | FC-CONN-08 FC-CONN-10 FC-INST-05 | partial | Status, automatic reconnect, Retry now, doctor. A lost link also ends the grants (PM1), and one button in the sidebar gives them again. |
| [24-F09](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#extension-signed-in-to-a-different-organization) | Organization matching | FC-CONN-26 | none | No account in the extension (D4). The Windows user is the boundary. |
| [24-F10](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#manage-site-permissions) | Site permissions | FC-GRANT-02 FC-GRANT-05 FC-GRANT-07 FC-GRANT-09 | partial | Stricter: a grant is per tab and origin and made by the user, and sensitive calls ask each time (PM7). One answer for all actions on a site is not offered. |
| [24-F11](https://claude.com/claude-in-chrome) | Permission modes | FC-GRANT-03 FC-GRANT-12 FC-GRANT-08 | partial | Levels read and act, and approvals. A mode that skips the checks is excluded. |
| [24-F12](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#browser-tools-in-plan-mode) | Plan-mode prompts | FC-GRANT-12 FC-SHOT-14 FC-TAB-08 FC-AGT-04 | different | Recordings and new origins always ask in extension UI. The client's plan mode can use the tool annotations; Companion does not see the mode. |
| [24-F13](https://claude.com/claude-in-chrome) | Sidebar | FC-UI-01 FC-UI-02 FC-AGT-02 FC-AGT-09 | different | Firefox sidebar with the user's own Claude Code, skills and connectors. The product page gives no mechanics to compare. |
| [24-F14](https://claude.com/claude-in-chrome) | Session history | FC-UI-03 | partial | History stays on this computer. Resuming on other devices would need an account (D4). |
| [24-F15](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Visible window and tab group | FC-TAB-21 FC-TAB-11 | different | Firefox tab groups. Tabs stay open at the end by default, so that no page of the user is lost. |
| [24-F16](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#extension-signed-in-to-a-different-organization) | Shortcuts | FC-AGT-10 | different | Saved prompts in the extension. The benchmark's mechanics are not described. |
| [24-F17](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#extension-signed-in-to-a-different-organization) | Scheduled and background tasks | FC-AGT-11 FC-TAB-11 FC-UI-14 | partial | Background work during a session, yes. Schedules are deferred: grants need the user, and runs end with Firefox (P13). |
| [24-F18](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#record-a-demo-gif) | GIF recording of a workflow | FC-SHOT-14 FC-UI-11 | full | With a start approval, covered secret fields and a review note. Needs `<all_urls>` for background tabs. |
| [24-F19](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#save-screenshots-to-disk) | Save screenshots to disk | FC-SHOT-09 | full | Into the session folder, never over an existing file. |
| [24-F20](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Login and CAPTCHA handling | FC-ACT-29 FC-ACT-30 FC-AGT-14 | full | Detection is Companion's own, because the benchmark's is not described. |
| [24-F21](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#browser-not-responding) | Blocked page recovery | FC-ACT-25 FC-ACT-26 FC-CONN-10 | full | Dialogs during an action do not block. A native dialog that is already open must be closed by the user, as in the benchmark. The link is restarted from the sidebar instead of an extensions page. |
| [24-F22](https://claude.com/claude-in-chrome) | Prompt-injection safety | FC-GRANT-25 FC-READ-25 FC-GRANT-10 FC-AGT-01 | different | Fixed rules outside the model. The benchmark's safeguards are claimed without details, so they cannot be compared. |
| [24-F23](https://claude.com/claude-in-chrome) | Sensitive workflows | FC-GRANT-13 FC-GRANT-12 | full | Enforced warning and extra approvals instead of advice only. |
| [24-F24](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#chrome-availability-with-hipaa-or-zero-data-retention) | Admin controls | FC-GRANT-26 FC-GRANT-01 | partial | Site lists of the user, yes. Organization rules are deferred, because policy delivery to the add-on was not verified. |
| [24-F25](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#project-settings-cant-turn-on-chrome) | Project settings guard | FC-GRANT-23 FC-CONN-30 | full | No project file can loosen a rule. |
| [24-F26](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#upload-files-to-web-pages) | Local file upload workflow | FC-ACT-21 | partial | 10 MB, hard links, credential names and folders are covered. Companion does not read Claude Code's deny rules for Read; the allowed folders take their place. |
| [24-F27](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#example-workflows) | Authenticated workflows | FC-TAB-39 FC-ACT-29 FC-ACT-08 | partial | The signed-in profile is the purpose of the product. Editors and pages that need trusted input may not respond (FC-ACT-01), and none of the named sites was tried. |
| [24-F28](https://claude.com/claude-in-chrome) | Cross-app examples | FC-AGT-05 FC-READ-23 FC-ACT-21 | partial | Claimed only. The browser part is covered; connectors belong to the user's Claude Code, and no success criteria are given. |
| [24-F29](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Tab scoping (`tabs_context_mcp`) | FC-TAB-01 FC-TAB-07 FC-TAB-28 FC-GRANT-02 | different | Grants and session tabs decide instead of a tab group. A user tab can be granted too. |
| [24-F30](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Tab creation (`tabs_create_mcp`) | FC-TAB-08 | full | Also opens an address, with an approval for a new origin. |
| [24-F31](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Tab close (`tabs_close_mcp`) | FC-TAB-14 | full | User tabs need an approval. |
| [24-F32](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Navigation (`navigate`) | FC-TAB-17 FC-TAB-18 FC-TAB-12 | full | Leaving the origin asks first. |
| [24-F33](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Input and screenshots (`computer`) | FC-ACT-01 FC-ACT-02 FC-ACT-03 FC-ACT-07 FC-ACT-08 FC-ACT-10 FC-ACT-17 FC-ACT-18 FC-ACT-19 FC-ACT-32 FC-ACT-34 FC-SHOT-01 FC-SHOT-04 FC-SHOT-09 | partial | All actions are planned. Input is synthetic, so pages that check trusted events ignore it, and `:hover` is not set. Captures need `<all_urls>` or a toolbar click. |
| [24-F34](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Accessibility tree (`read_page`) | FC-READ-01 FC-READ-02 FC-READ-03 | full | Frames of other origins need their own grant. |
| [24-F35](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Element search (`find`) | FC-READ-07 | partial | Word ranking in the extension. The benchmark's natural-language method is not described, and no model runs in the extension. |
| [24-F36](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Form input (`form_input`) | FC-ACT-12 FC-ACT-13 FC-ACT-32 | partial | Secret fields are refused (PM8), so the user fills them in. |
| [24-F37](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Page text (`get_page_text`) | FC-READ-10 FC-READ-11 | full | Own extraction of the main article. |
| [24-F38](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Page JavaScript (`javascript_tool`) | FC-DBG-09 FC-DBG-10 FC-DBG-11 | none | Code supplied at run time conflicts with P2 and P4 in a signed add-on. Typed page queries cover common reads. |
| [24-F39](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Console messages (`read_console_messages`) | FC-DBG-01 FC-DBG-02 FC-DBG-03 | partial | Text filters with `*` instead of regular expressions, because an agent's expression could hang the extension. Output before capture started is missing. |
| [24-F40](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Network requests (`read_network_requests`) | FC-DBG-04 FC-GRANT-20 | partial | Same filters and redaction. Requests to origins without host permission are not seen. |
| [24-F41](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | File upload (`file_upload`) | FC-ACT-21 FC-ACT-22 | partial | The same limits, but allowed folders instead of the paths that the session may read (see [24-F26](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome#upload-files-to-web-pages)). |
| [24-F42](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Image upload (`upload_image`) | FC-ACT-24 | full | Image IDs last 5 minutes. |
| [24-F43](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | GIF creator (`gif_creator`) | FC-SHOT-14 | full | Same overlays and quality range. |
| [24-F44](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Window resize (`resize_window`) | FC-TAB-30 FC-TAB-31 | full | Also position and state. |
| [24-F45](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Batched calls (`browser_batch`) | FC-AGT-05 | full | All steps are checked before the first runs. |
| [24-F46](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Connected browser list | FC-CONN-01 FC-CONN-17 | partial | Profiles of this Windows user only, because there is no account bridge. |
| [24-F47](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Browser selection (`select_browser`) | FC-CONN-01 FC-CONN-17 | partial | The same limit as [24-F46](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome). |
| [24-F48](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome) | Browser switch request (`switch_browser`) | FC-CONN-17 | partial | The same limit as [24-F46](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/chrome). |

## Open questions

Each item says what is unknown, and what depends on it. Nothing here was tested.

For the user:

1. Answered on 2026-11-24: the user approved D1 to D9 with clarifications ("Decisions").
2. Pick the public name, after Mozilla's naming requirements are verified (D9, P7, P8).
3. May a claude.ai subscription back the sidebar chat through the user's own Claude Code? This was not verified. FC-UI-02 and FC-AGT-16 depend on it. The MCP bridge does not. Found on 2026-11-24 ([Legal and compliance](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/legal-and-compliance)): a person may sign in to the unmodified Claude Code with their own subscription; a third party may not offer claude.ai login, route requests through plan credentials for its users, or hold those credentials; running Claude Code inside a product needs the Commercial Terms and the conditions on that page. On 2026-11-24 `claude -p` without `--bare` ran with the user's own claude.ai sign-in (Claude Code 2.1.302). Still open for a public release, and for the user to decide. Also: the [headless page](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/headless) says that `--bare`, which never reads a subscription login, will become the default of `claude -p`.
4. Listed or self-distributed add-on? FC-INST-08, FC-INST-10 and FC-INST-12 cover both, and one must be chosen.

For Mozilla review:

5. How do AMO reviewers judge an AI agent that uses native messaging, and the optional `<all_urls>`? FC-INST-08 and FC-SHOT-01 depend on it.
6. Does the policy revision of 2026-12-01 (P8) change more than P2 to P5 and P7? Read it again before the first submission.
7. Will a question about agent-supplied JavaScript be judged as D5 expects? FC-DBG-09 and FC-DBG-11 depend on it.

For a run on Firefox 160 (no source or document settles these):

8. Can a download that starts after a click be cancelled so that no file is left, and be told apart from the user's own (FC-ACT-01)?
9. Can a window that page script opened be closed within 1 s, before its page loads content, without disturbing the user (FC-ACT-05)?
10. Does the rectangle option of tabs.captureVisibleTab reach outside the visible area (FC-SHOT-04)? The rows assume that it does not.
11. Not needed: the manifest names a program (Prompt 2), and Firefox 160.0.1 starts it without a window. Not tested: does Firefox start a batch file named in a native manifest ([06-F54](https://github.com/raychao-oao/firefox-bridge/blob/e7bfb049801075500e5ddd7fb5cdbfe689f1feff/scripts/install-native-manifest.js#L22-L34)), and which program type avoids a console window (FC-INST-07)?
12. Answered by Prompt 2: the host is C#, built with the compiler of Windows. It calls CreateNamedPipeW with a security descriptor for the current user, PIPE_REJECT_REMOTE_CLIENTS and FILE_FLAG_FIRST_PIPE_INSTANCE (FC-CONN-04).
13. Which permissions does Firefox 160 accept as optional in MV2, in particular webRequest with blocking, downloads, find, cookies, tabGroups and clipboardWrite (FC-INST-19, FC-DBG-05)? Answered by Prompt 2 on 160.0.1: accepted are `<all_urls>`, activeTab, bookmarks, browserSettings, browsingData, clipboardRead, clipboardWrite, cookies, devtools, downloads, downloads.open, find, geolocation, history, idle, management, menus.overrideContext, nativeMessaging, notifications, pkcs11, privacy, proxy, scripting, search, sessions, tabGroups, tabHide, tabs, topSites, webNavigation, webRequest, webRequestBlocking and webRequestFilterResponse. Refused are alarms, captivePortal, contextualIdentities, menus, pageCapture, storage, theme, unlimitedStorage and userScripts. This is what the manifest accepts; a prompt was shown only for host permissions. FC-INST-19 lists contextualIdentities as optional, which does not work.
14. Answered by Prompt 2: yes, the toolbar button gives activeTab although its handler opens the sidebar (it was open already). Still open: Does Firefox let a click on a notification open the sidebar (FC-UI-14)?
15. Do hooks at navigation commit miss console output of the first moments (FC-DBG-01), and can a page notice the exported functions (FC-DBG-07)?
16. Do synthetic events drive the editors and drag targets of the sites that the user cares about (FC-ACT-08, FC-ACT-19)? A list of sites is needed.
17. Can a closed tab be reopened without taking the focus (FC-DATA-15), and can a new window be opened without focus (FC-TAB-11)?
18. Does a chat run end cleanly with the host's job object (P13, FC-CONN-24)?
19. Claude Code 2.1.302 declares the roots capability and answers roots/list with its launch folder (observed on 2026-11-24, also in the [MCP page](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/mcp)). Read-only and idempotent hints have an effect there by that page. Still open: whether it shows annotations and resource links to the user (FC-ACT-21, FC-AGT-04, FC-SHOT-09). It writes a text result over 50,000 characters to a file, so read_page defaults to 45,000.
20. Can a native host wake a stopped event page? D1 treats "no" as an inference. It matters only if Manifest V3 is considered.

For the advanced run:

21. Which of the BiDi commands that the sources use (moz:debugging, moz:profiler, screencast, webExtension) does stable Firefox offer, and which need system access, Marionette or Nightly? [13-F24](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/src/tools/webextension.ts#L31-L63) and [13-F40](https://github.com/mozilla/firefox-mcp/blob/02c5df84e6ec492be07796479df3883ce462e33c/plugins/firefox-mcp/skills/web-performance/SKILL.md#L1-L61) name version floors, and [03-F18](https://github.com/drubino-mozilla/agent-f/blob/9e87e4bec424da3e782fe850e5fed292813aeb12/broker/agent_f/tools_webdriver.py#L195-L299) says that its remote control is for Nightly only (FC-DBG-12, FC-DBG-13, FC-DBG-14, FC-SHOT-15, FC-ACT-36).
22. The remote agent of the isolated Firefox opens a local port, which D2 does not allow in the everyday product. How may the backend use and protect it (FC-CONN-28)?

## Audit limits

- The audit ran nothing on Firefox 160 or any other Firefox. Prompt 2 ran the built slice there. No source was installed, built, run or tested. Statements about sources come from reading code at the pinned commits. Statements about Firefox come from the pinned documents in "Platform prerequisites".
- Of the 913 findings, 770 are marked verified, 134 partial and 9 claimed-only. Verified means that a worker read the registration and the handler; it does not mean that the behavior was observed. Partial findings name what is missing or broken, and the rows use these as defects to avoid.
- Findings were written by several workers, and checked by a tool for form, links and line ranges. Their content was checked by spot checks only, so not every finding has a second reader, and a wrong finding can have reached a row. Open the pinned link before relying on a detail.
- This document was written from the digests, which cut the behavior at 520 characters. Full findings were opened only for some cut lines. Check the full finding in `docs/audit/` for limits and numbers of a source.
- Source 24 is known from a documentation page, a product page and recorded tool schemas. No tool was called and no code was seen, so coverage is against its description.
- Each findings file lists unread or unchecked items under "Claimed but not verified". Larger ones: protocol, API and configuration documents of 05 and 14; `thunderbird/mail.js` and `thunderbird/experiments/mcpChrome` of 05; design documents and the end-to-end harness of 10; popup or options pages of 04, 17, 18 and 20; documents and scripts of 13; the website and worker of 11; the separate extension repository that 22 mentions; test files or fixtures of 01, 08 and 09; security documents of 04.
- Store listings, published packages, signing states, Cloudflare services and remote relays were not checked, because the audit used no network. Claims about them stay claims ([08-F35](https://github.com/jhueby/claude-browser-bridge/blob/58749d978a26d7b675225d15d6b17591034ab936/README.md#L63), [09-F41](https://github.com/thriftshoppin/claude-firefox-bridge/blob/65f7dc32b05fe09f76c2d4bc1774e68c127f3ee0/amo/metadata.json#L1-L24), [16-F38](https://github.com/dh1011/firefox-mcp/blob/64212a5988eb3384a70ec1e6c88428e65d8ec39c/docs/how-it-works.md#L73-L79)).
- Licenses were read in the repositories. Whether a fork relation that a source states is complete was not checked (16 and Browser MCP; 17 and 18).
- The security defects of sources were found by reading. No attack was tried, for example whether a web page can reach the open sockets of 19, 20 and 23.
- Limits, time spans and sizes in the rows (for example 120 calls per minute, 60 s for a card) are proposals from the best reference or from this project's judgment. None was measured.
- The matrix has more rows than the 130 to 190 first expected, because capabilities that can fail separately have separate rows.
- The checks behind this document are formal: every finding is carried, links are the pinned ones, rows are complete, references resolve. They do not judge whether a row describes its sources well.
